# Skills · L · part 91

891 entries, log-investigation to logfire. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **log-investigation**: Investigate a reported failure or recurring problem in the logs using testable hypotheses and evidence (root-cause analysis). Use when the question asks WHY or WHAT is causing an issue, not for direct counts, lists, or lookups. ([KaisatoK/loractor](https://github.com/KaisatoK/loractor/tree/HEAD/skills/log-investigation))
- **log-investigation**: Investigate server logs fast - summarise nginx/Apache access logs (status codes, top IPs, paths, user agents, 5xx and 404 hot spots, busiest minutes, scanner probes, slowest endpoints) with a script that reads rotated .gz files, plus journalctl, grep and awk recipes for systemd services, auth.log/SSH, kernel, PHP-FPM, MySQL and Docker logs. Use when the user asks what is in the logs, why errors or 5xx spiked, who is hammering the site, which IP or bot to block, what happened at a certain time, or to find the error behind a failing service. ([saizul/vps-devops-skills](https://github.com/saizul/vps-devops-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/log-investigation))
- **log-investigator**: Investigate application failures from logs in local processes, containers, and Kubernetes. Use this whenever the user mentions logs, `stern`, `kubectl logs`, error bursts, missing traces, or wants to correlate a failure across services or repos under `~/dune/`, especially when the owning repo or worktree is unclear. ([msf/configs](https://github.com/msf/configs/tree/HEAD/ai-agents/skills/log-investigator) · ★1)
- **log-investigator**: Recall what the user and Claude did in past sessions by searching Claude Code transcripts (~/.claude/projects/*/*.jsonl) at 3 cheap-to-expensive levels instead of reading raw logs. Use when the user asks "what did we do last time / yesterday / last week with X", "what did we decide about Y", "find where we discussed Z", "which session did …", or when context was lost and past decisions, commands or file changes need to be reconstructed. Also use before re-asking the user something that may already have been decided in an earlier session. ([dtngan/claude-recall](https://github.com/dtngan/claude-recall/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **log-investor-meeting**: Log an investor meeting into Portal's Airtable VC CRM using the Otter.ai transcript. Use this whenever someone says they just met with, spoke to, or had a call with a VC firm or investor and wants it logged, summarized, or added to Airtable — e.g. "I just met with Accel, check Otter and update Airtable", "log my Benchmark call", "pull my Otter notes from the Sequoia meeting into the CRM". Also use it when someone asks to update an investor's Airtable record with meeting notes, even if they don't mention Otter by name. ([chilampoon/portal-claude-plugins](https://github.com/chilampoon/portal-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/investor-meeting-summary/skills/log-investor-meeting))
- **log-io-taking-longer**: Performs root cause analysis (RCA) for Soc100.6 "Log I/O taking longer" incidents in Hyperscale (Socrates) databases by analyzing error 833 on Compute, Log Replica health, HADR replica states, and Fabric API role changes. Use this skill when investigating long log I/O stalls, error 833 on Compute or LogReplica, or Soc100.6 IcM alerts. ([sqlcss/copilot-agents](https://github.com/sqlcss/copilot-agents/tree/HEAD/.github/agents/skills/kql-templates/sqldb/socrates/log-service-rcas/log-io-taking-longer) · ★1)
- **log-issue**: Log any GitHub issue (bug, feature, or task) via `gh issue create` using the canonical issue body structure — TL;DR and Details for humans, Steps To Reproduce, Details (for AI), Acceptance Criteria (for AI), and Screenshots. Use when the user says "log an issue", "file an issue", "create an issue", "log a bug", or "report a bug", and when backfilling existing issue bodies to the canonical structure. ([Nimblesite/Deslop](https://github.com/Nimblesite/Deslop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/log-issue) · ★75)
- **log-issue**: File a high-quality, verified GitHub issue for goaly using the matching template. Classifies the report (bug / feature / enhancement / harness adapter / discussion). For BUGS it enforces reported → replicated → logged: the bug is reproduced before it is filed, and the report carries verified reproduction steps plus a preliminary cause. For features/enhancements it captures a comprehensive, mission-and-invariant-aware proposal. Use when the user wants to report a bug, request a feature, propose an enhancement, request a harness, or open a discussion for this repo. ([krimvp/goaly](https://github.com/krimvp/goaly/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/log-issue) · ★2)
- **log-issue**: Use when the owner describes a bug or feature idea to log as a GitHub issue, answers an issue's open questions, or asks what's ready to build next. - **log-quality-scan**: 扫描日志文件或代码，逐一检查每条日志是否符合日志标准（分级/描述/组件/防刷屏/链路追踪/隐私），输出通过/不通过清单和改进建议。触发词：扫描日志、判断日志好坏、日志质量检查、日志扫描。 ([xiaoshudian555/private-skills](https://github.com/xiaoshudian555/private-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/log-quality/log-quality-scan) · ★3)
- **log-quality-scan-code**: 从代码仓库中扫描日志语句，直接检查每条日志是否符合 log-quality-standard，并检测关键位置是否遗漏日志记录，输出按标准分组的不合规问题清单。触发词：扫描代码仓日志、结合代码仓分析日志质量、代码日志质量检查、检查关键路径是否有日志。 ([xiaoshudian555/private-skills](https://github.com/xiaoshudian555/private-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/log-quality/log-quality-scan-code) · ★3)
- **log-quality-standard**: 日志标准规范的适配器层。根据外部标准文档执行日志质量检查，调用 scan / rewrite / write 能力。触发词：日志标准、日志规范、日志质量标准、log standard、log quality、制定日志规范。 ([xiaoshudian555/private-skills](https://github.com/xiaoshudian555/private-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/log-quality/log-quality-standard) · ★3)
